CORE AND SHARED UI CAPABILITY INVENTORY
Date: 2026-11-17 (Asia/Calcutta); recovery review, read-only application inspection.

SCOPE AND EVIDENCE LIMITS
All current core/ui source, tests, configs and declarations were enumerated and full text scanned for exports, imports, test declarations and known incomplete-code indicators. Detailed manual excerpts covered core public barrels, core/UI package manifests, action executor/validator, timeline managers/auto edit, playback controller/master-clock relationships, MediaBunny/import pipeline, renderer factory/video/export engines, WebCodecs/worker relationship, animation/easing/keyframe variants, expression paths, AI/model loading, segmentation fallback, optical flow/upscale, multicam/OTIO, creation module barrels/runtime and shared UI exports/components.
This is an architectural capability audit, not a line-by-line semantic/security proof for every implementation. The per-file appendix states what was statically indexed. No application code or configuration was changed; no unknown scripts or archive programs were executed. No tests were rerun by this audit. Prior onboarding task recorded successful workspace typecheck/build and core unit tests; that cannot establish all listed UI/hardware behavior. Every feature below needs its stated acceptance gap checked.
Presence = implementation exists. Nearby tests = actual test file, not a claim it passed now. A public barrel or capability manifest is not evidence that a feature is exposed, connected, licensed or production-ready.

ACTIVE ARCHITECTURE / OWNERSHIP
Web UI -> project-store and bridges -> ActionExecutor/registered handlers -> one Project/Timeline schema. EngineStore supplies PlaybackController -> MasterTimelineClock + RealtimeAudioGraph; VideoEngine supplies decoded/rendered frames. Toolbar/DesktopExportButton -> services/export-runner -> ExportEngine -> injected EncoderBackend (WebCodecsBackend in browser; native adapter in desktop). Export consumes the same VideoEngine/AudioEngine and project data. MotionComposition and Project.creation are embedded content, not permission to instantiate a second global editing timeline.
Keep @openreel/core as domain/runtime owner; @openreel/ui as shared presentational primitives; web stores/controllers own user interaction. Adapters may differ for desktop/browser, while timing, serialization, history and action identity remain shared.

SPECIFIC DUPLICATE / DISCONNECTED CANDIDATES AND COMPATIBLE CONSOLIDATION
D1. video/playback-engine.ts versus playback/playback-controller.ts. Former owns separate currentTime/rAF/MediaBunny buffer streams, latter is the active EngineStore/web bridge clock with RealtimeAudioGraph. Source search found no current consumer of former outside definitions/barrel. Verify all workspace/dynamic imports; move old implementation unchanged into AnalyseFuture with origin/reason and remove only obsolete public export if no consumers. Do not wire two clocks into one editor.
D2. export/export-worker.ts versus WebCodecsBackend. Worker duplicates MediaBunny Output/VideoSampleSource/AudioSampleSource, codec selection, cancellation and buffering/chunk messages. Active ExportEngine imports WebCodecsBackend; no worker construction/import found. Preserve obsolete worker and nullable exportWorker field only after full-reference confirmation; retain EncoderBackend for any later worker acceleration so audio priming/limits/written frame accounting remain single logic.
D3. video/unified-effects-processor.ts versus WebGPUEffectsProcessor/VideoEngine. UnifiedEffectsProcessor requests a GPU device but processWithWebGPU delegates directly to Canvas2D; no active caller found. Preserve legacy module, route future feature discovery through actual renderer capability. Never label this path GPU accelerated.
D4. animation/composition-renderer.ts plus types/composition.ts versus motion/MotionComposition. The former uses independent layer schema, duplicates keyframe/transform/blend logic, no production caller found, and group layer render case is empty. Preserve historical renderer; if importers use the schema keep a versioned converter into MotionComposition rather than remove exported types blindly.
D5. AnimationEngine/KeyframeEngine/composition-renderer and GSAP interpolation contain overlap, but existing evaluateKeyframesAt already delegates to KeyframeEngine. Consolidate common numeric/object/Bezier/hold evaluator and easing registry behind public compatibility APIs; test all curve cases before relocating code. Motion keyframe descriptors/expression bindings are domain adapters, not disposable duplicates.
D6. effects/expression-engine.ts and motion/motion-expressions.ts both compile custom JS via new Function. The former appears unused, the latter is active motion capability with cycle/depth guards. These guards are not a JS sandbox. Preserve unused engine and centralize any future interpreter/isolated worker budget; never execute AI/untrusted project expressions solely because a safeContext object exists.
D7. audio/volume-automation.ts and clip-volume-automation.ts differ: offline AudioBuffer processing/ducking/Bezier curves versus source-offset cropping and GainNode scheduling. Both should reuse clamp/evaluation semantics; do not remove one as a duplicate without effect/curve parity coverage.
D8. template/TemplateEngine, editing-templates recipe resolver and motion templates have different abstraction levels. Converge validation/control schemas and undoable command application, while maintaining separate versioned document/clip/composition formats.
D9. core/photo and image-core both support image work; core/photo is referenced by current web photo-bridge/project-store. Do not replace with the separate image app or introduce competing project storage. Compare capability contracts and add asset export/import bridge only after acceptance tests.
D10. Multiple GPU/Canvas/Three/native render backends are legitimate, not automatically duplicate engines. Retain backend adapters behind shared Project/MotionComposition timing and render contracts; evaluate actual output/driver coverage.
D11. UI generic and Toolcraft wrappers overlap visually but expose different props/design semantics; consolidate tokens and primitives through compatible wrappers, do not bulk swap imports without accessibility/interaction evidence.

SECURITY / CLAIM LIMITATIONS
- No evidence in this audit establishes who changed GitHub or proves a compromise. Unexpected/imported files are classified by content and connectivity only.
- Custom expression new Function is a concrete security-review prerequisite for untrusted project/AI execution; model workers and shaders need resource/isolation budgets too.
- Person segmentation model URLs include mutable latest paths on Google storage; MediaPipe JS/WASM use unpkg/jsDelivr. Silero VAD uses pinned model repository version but artifact integrity/licensing/cache still need verification. Local-first does not imply first-run offline availability.
- Browser SpeechRecognition may depend on remote provider/browser implementation. Do not market core SpeechToTextEngine as guaranteed offline Whisper.
- BackgroundRemovalEngine catches model init failure, sets useAI false and still initialized true. A usable engine state can therefore coexist with degraded non-AI behavior; UI/export must communicate model availability.
- Upscaling is Lanczos/edge-directed/sharpening; optical-flow interpolation can fall back to simple blending. Neither reconstructs provably true details or guarantees artifact-free frame creation.
- OTIO output is generated standards-shaped JSON; no successful external-editor/library round trip established by this audit.
- Device/profile heuristics, capability lists and unit mocks do not validate Windows, GPU/4K speed, film-grade rendering, native rigging fidelity or professional editor parity.

FUNCTIONAL OWNERSHIP REVIEW — STRUCTURE ONLY
Client date: 17 November 2026
Scope: active non-web apps/packages, deployment boundaries, Studio prototype references,
and inventory-classification quality. No tests, builds, type checks, installations,
archive execution, Git mutations or application edits were performed for this review.

1. REPOSITORY BOUNDARIES THAT SHOULD REMAIN

editor/ is a real pnpm workspace, not an arbitrary bucket. Its pnpm-workspace.yaml
selects apps/* and packages/* relative to editor/. Those paths should stay because
workspace dependencies, Vite aliases, TypeScript paths and native packaging rely
on them. apps are executable surfaces; packages hold reusable responsibilities.
Keeping these framework conventions is compatible with functional organization.

AnalyseFuture/reference-sources contains independent source snapshots and should
remain outside this workspace selection. Root scripts/tests are project-level QA
and repository maintenance, not part of any browser bundle. Active .github workflows
are repository automation, not application feature code. Do not fold them into src/.

Production/package exceptions are functional, not extension-based:
- apps/web/public and apps/image/public are URL-served assets. Moving them into
  arbitrary image/font folders can break URLs and offline behavior.
- apps/desktop/resources/{bin,rigging,aurora}, LICENSES and build are explicitly
  named by electron-builder.yml. Resources carry native/runtime responsibilities;
  LICENSES carries distribution obligations; build carries packaging metadata.
- apps/web/functions is framework-discovered server/API infrastructure. Its path
  must remain until a supported deployment rewrite is designed.
- Package entrypoints and exports are public integration boundaries. No caller
  found in a simple search is insufficient reason to remove an exported API.
- Existing core domain directories already group functional behavior. They are
  not extension-only directories and should not all be flattened or merged.

2. NON-WEB FEATURE / RESPONSIBILITY TAXONOMY

Presence below means implementation was found, not verified behavior in this pass.
Keep package-local tests adjacent where present; do not move them into an unrelated
global test bucket merely because their extension contains .test.

CORE — editor/packages/core/src/
01 actions/: validation, execution, serialization, inverse commands and undo groups.
02 timeline/, editing/: clip/track/sequence editing and timeline operations.
03 media/: import, track extraction, metadata, codecs and media identity.
04 playback/: master clock, playback controller and preview scheduling.
05 audio/: routing, mixing, fades, effects, FFT, beat/silence analysis and sound library.
06 video/: rendering, decode, frame processing and transitions/effects backends.
07 export/: encode/mux/export orchestration and backend boundaries.
08 storage/: project serialization and storage responsibility.
09 text/, graphics/: titles/captions, vector/shape/graphic capability.
10 effects/, animation/: effect contracts, expressions, keyframe evaluation and easing.
11 motion/, creation/: motion/scene/geometry behavior, distinct from application UI.
12 ai/: segmentation and reframing engines; not the general LLM transport layer.
13 multicam/: camera/multicamera contracts and processing.
14 photo/: still-image manipulation primitives.
15 template/, editing-templates/: reusable editing/template contracts.
16 wasm/: AssemblyScript/native acceleration adapters, not a separate editor model.
17 capabilities/, device/, types/, utils/: cross-domain contracts and runtime support.

SHARED UI — editor/packages/ui/src/
18 components/: Radix-backed controls and Toolcraft design-system controls.
19 styles/, lib/: shared styling and utility contracts.
Decision: keep package boundaries; web and companion apps consume shared controls.
Do not move reusable UI into one app's private feature folder.

AGENT / HEADLESS
20 packages/agent/src/{registry,tool-router,executor,host,loop,serialize,observability}:
   tool registration, validated execution/host integration and interaction loop.
21 packages/agent/src/{llm,system-prompt,headless-host,creation-product-motion}:
   model boundary, headless integration and creation-aware tooling.
22 packages/agent-runner/src/{cli,run,project-io,node-llm,export-queue,evals}:
   Node/CLI execution, persistence, model transport and queued exports/evaluation.
Decision: different runtimes, not interchangeable copies. agent-runner depends on
agent/core; merging it into browser source would introduce Node APIs into the UI.

CREATION / NATIVE / EFFECT ARTIFACTS
23 packages/creation-schema/src/{types,primitives,validate,product-cinematic}:
   declarative scene/creation schema and validation; dependency-light contract.
24 packages/creation-core/{src,include}: C++ native ABI and Aurora rendering code.
25 packages/creation-bindings/{src,native,wasm}: native discovery/binding and CPU/WASM
   fallbacks; public API exposes selected backend and render kind explicitly.
26 packages/creation-agent/src/{types,tools}: creation schema tools; complementary
   to agent's broader host/router rather than a second authoritative video project.
27 packages/fxpkg/src/{abi,nodelib,validator,manifest,package,blueprints,compiler}:
   effect artifact contract, graph validation and filter/template compilation.
28 packages/image-core/src/{schema,project,commands,operations,adjustments,selection,
   mask,migration}: still-image project/model operations; separate media responsibility.

DESKTOP — editor/apps/desktop/src/
29 main/{index,lifecycle,app-menu,window-controls,protocol,nav-guard,updater}:
   Electron app lifecycle, protocol/window behavior and update boundary.
30 main/ipc/: validated renderer/native message handlers, filesystem, keychain,
   media, hardware, cloud access and export interfaces.
31 main/sidecar/: FFmpeg export/media jobs, codec argument construction, stream
   probing, rigging/Blender integration and executable selection.
32 main/mcp/, mcp-shim/: external protocol server and stdio integration shim.
33 main/aurora/, aurora-host/: native rendering process/client boundary.
34 preload/, shared/: renderer capability exposure and shared IPC contracts.
Decision: preserve process boundaries. A filesystem IPC handler does not belong
inside browser media UI just because both feature names contain 'media'.

CLOUD — editor/apps/cloud/src/
35 index.ts: Hono application entrypoint and legacy templates/share/highlight routes.
36 marketplace/{marketplace,studio,events,auth,db,storage,types}: marketplace public
   catalogue and creator/review/event support backed by worker bindings.
37 crash/routes.ts: crash ingestion/access boundary.
Decision: optional remote service, not required for core local editing. Keep it
separate from browser feature code. Current header-based development identity and
unguarded legacy mutations are security concerns, not proof of account compromise;
earlier unfinished remediations are preserved proposals, not delivered changes.

IMAGE — editor/apps/image/src/
38 App.tsx + components/{welcome,editor}: companion image workflow and editor shell.
39 adjustments/, effects/, filters/, tools/: actual still-image editing operations.
40 stores/, services/, types/, utils/: image project/history/assets/export/persistence.
Decision: its artboards, image layers and history solve a different responsibility
from the video timeline. Do not merge stores merely because both are named project.

STUDIO — editor/apps/studio/src/
41 effect/: active stateful scene/effect editor with store, inspectors, behaviors,
   preview, particles, compilation and persistence helpers.
42 filter/: filter adjustments and stack authoring.
43 hub/: preset resolution, drafts and entry/navigation surfaces.
44 publish/: marketplace publication transport and artifact assembly interface.
45 tutorials/: tutorial content, context and overlay.
46 components/template/TemplateAuthor.tsx: active template author UI using shared
   data.ts; several static/placeholder actions mean source presence is not completion.
47 e2e/ and e2e-web/ outside src/: existing Studio and main-editor browser acceptance
   suites. Keep their real app working directories and configuration relationship.

3. EXACT DISCONNECTED STUDIO PROTOTYPE CANDIDATE

Active import: apps/studio/src/App.tsx imports './effect/EffectEditor' at line 5 and
mounts that implementation with initialSeed/initialScene. The active module uses
effect/store, SceneTree, Preview, Inspector and BehaviorPaletteSheet.

Separate old UI: apps/studio/src/components/effect/EffectEditor.tsx (662 lines) uses
static FIRE_GRAPH, GRAPH_PERF and PALETTE from ../../data. It imports './FireScene'.
FireScene.tsx is referenced by that old module; the search across Studio source
found no importer of the old components/effect/EffectEditor module from App or
other runtime modules. These are a disconnected prototype pair, not two equally
active editors. They can be preserved under AnalyseFuture/duplicate-implementations/
with original relative paths after root performs its whole-repository reference
check. No public package export for this pair was found.

Do not move apps/studio/src/data.ts with the pair: the active TemplateAuthor imports
TRAVEL_TEMPLATE and SlotKind from ../../data. A filename-overlap cleanup that moves
all static data would break an active workflow. This is an example of dependency-
based grouping rather than sorting by names.

4. CONCRETE CAPABILITY LIMITS NOT TO MISREPORT

- creation-bindings/src/wasm.ts embeds a constant box_vertex_count WASM module.
  loadWasmBackend reports renderKind:'cpu' and delegates mesh rendering to the TS
  CPU raytracer. This is not evidence of full native/WASM renderer parity.
- image/services/export-service.ts includes svg/pdf in ExportFormat but its output
  path chooses jpg/webp/otherwise PNG canvas encoding. No vector serializer was
  found in that service; do not advertise vector/PDF export based only on the union.
- Desktop builder settings include upstream developer identity/update host details.
  Their presence is not this project's release identity or proof of verified native
  signing/support. Do not change them as an incidental structure-only edit; record
  ownership/configuration debt before any production distribution.
- Core declared acceleration/backends and native binaries need provenance/platform
  validation. Folder organization does not establish safety or runtime correctness.

5. INVENTORY CLASSIFICATION AND REFERENCE CAUTIONS

scripts/repository/audit_repository.py correctly discloses path/static inference, but its
original classify() uses only the first directory after src/. After parent moves
web to src/features/<name>/, that would classify all domains as 'features'. Extend
the classifier to recognize features/<domain>, shared and app layers. The generator
reads text and static imports, but does not prove full dynamic call-graph reachability.

Moves must account for package exports, tsconfig/Vite aliases, vi.mock/import string
references, new URL(relative, import.meta.url), tests loading source text, e2e page
imports such as /src/stores/project-store, and deployment-discovered paths. Do not
claim every runtime reference repaired solely from simple import regex success.

Source totals at inspection time (includes test/support files under src): desktop
48; cloud11; image129; studio86. These are source-tree file counts, not feature counts.
This report directly inspected manifests, public entrypoints, representative domain
implementation and packaging/route integration, and recursively enumerated source
paths. It is not a claim of line-by-line semantic review of every native/media/ML
algorithm or proof of complete feature discovery in inaccessible history. No behavior
was executed. A structure-only delivery must explicitly leave runtime checks unrun.
